It was lovely dining outside on Portage Creek and inside offered live music with a cozy, elegant atmosphere.
Our service was fast and friendly...wonderful off-season experience! 🥰

--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: e3ea7932-af41-4250-83a3-b0fc9002b232
entity_slug: ginny-lane-bar-and-grill
reviewer_name: Carol Lengerman
rating: 3
body: GREAT HAPPY HOUR MENU from 11-6pm

Shrimp special today: $20 all you can eat and comes with Cole slaw, hush puppies, and fries. Oysters were $1/apiece.  They were small but had good flavor.

Justin was a great waiter and we tipped him accordingly, but the bar service was unbelievably slow considering the staff to patron ratio was at least 2:1.

Great view of the ICW and yachts on the wharf.

This was not our first time here, and we were surprised at how run down it seemed.  With so many dining options in this area, they need to freshen up the place and motivate their staff.  No complaints about Justin.  Staff looked bored and uninterested.

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 39b494a8-8797-4755-9f51-7ad2e4e045db
entity_slug: ginny-lane-bar-and-grill
reviewer_name: Whitney Prince
rating: 5
body: We had a wonderful experience! My husband and his dad went fishing this morning on the pier and caught a cooler full of Red Drum, but we had no way to clean and cook it. GL has a "hook and cook" service so we called ahead and spoke to Clint. He was super helpful and told us to bring them in and they would clean and cook them for us. From the moment we walked in the door, everyone was so friendly and kind. Clint scooped up our cooler and took the fish to the back to clean and prep them himself. We started with raw oysters on the halfshell and cocktails The fish were served fried, blackened and grilled with 3 family style sides. We opted for the gouda mashed potatoes, mushroom risotto and green beans. What an amazing meal! Everything was wonderful and we could not have asked for better service. We will definitely be back again! Thanks Clint! You really took care of us today!

--- entity_reviews [row 5 of 5] ---
id: e79dfd2c-94cf-45eb-ab2a-6fea237de705
entity_slug: ginny-lane-bar-and-grill
reviewer_name: Nikki Reid
rating: 3
body: Our server Emmitt was AMAZING! He communicated with us constantly, and we had great conversations! My child ordered the popcorn shrimp and said it was gross. He loves popcorn shrimp so my husband tasted it, and he said it tasted like it came straight from Long John Silvers. My other son loved his gumbo. My husbands grouper was not good at all. My Prime Filet Mignon looks like someone took a meat cleaver to it, but it was not too bad. The green beans and mashed potatoes were so delicious. We did have to wait a while for our food, but with it being a holiday weekend we expected it. Our server definitely kept apologizing, and we reassured him it was no problem. I did not ice while we were waiting a huge tree roach scurry across the floor. We let our server know where it went (under a table full of people) and he walked over and watched it scurry back under the china cabinet against the wall. He said he doesn't do roaches so we all kind of laughed. I don't do roaches either, so I just kept my feet up. I will say it was storming, so I'm sure that's why the roach was inside. I don't know if we will be back with how not great the food was, but we have to see Emmitt in the future!
